- **applied-statistics**: Applied statistics for engineering decisions: power/sample-size planning, multiple-comparisons correction, bootstrap intervals on pass@k, judge agreement, non-determinism variance, confounding/Simpson's-paradox risk, error-model tolerance bands, sequential testing. - **appliedai**: Start AIDLC — the one-command entry point. Takes a problem statement and runs the FULL Applied AI Enterprise pipeline in autopilot via the orchestrator, through planning AND build, pausing only for HITL gate decisions, the cloud/stack choice, and connector credentials. Use whenever the user says "start AIDLC", "/appliedai" (or the legacy "/aidlc"), or drops a problem statement they want taken end-to-end. ([karthikbadri12/applied-ai-enterprise](https://github.com/karthikbadri12/applied-ai-enterprise/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/appliedai))
- **applier**: Navigates and submits a job application using Playwright MCP. Receives a tailored resume PDF and job listing. Handles account creation or login, fills all form fields with verified data, audits autofill for malformed values, skips cover letters entirely, applies the humanizer skill to any written free-text, and logs the outcome to applications.json. ([anochj/internship-finder](https://github.com/anochj/internship-finder/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/applier))
- **applier**: &gt; 本 skill 定义「结构化建议 → 可回滚代码改动」闭环中 **applier（实施方）** 的工作方法。 &gt; 建议 = proposer 产出（含断点定位 + 根因 + target_paths）；实施 = 本 skill 载入的 agent。 &gt; 你在编排者分配的 worktree 内把建议翻译成最小 diff；不改评价面、不读真值、不跑评测。 ([wyh2255/paper-research-assistant](https://github.com/wyh2255/paper-research-assistant/tree/HEAD/agentic-paper-search/agentic/evolution/applier))
